## In progress
- **Wave 8 started (2026-11-26, on the owner's word, in parallel with the tail of wave 7).** Design round only so far: six read-only designers (`opus`, background) write plans under `.artifacts/wave8/` (not tracked) against `.artifacts/wave8/brief.md`: `growing` «גדלים מול העיניים», `audiences` «גרסה לכל קהל», `ink` «ציור חי», `tripmap` «מפת טיול», `booth` «תא הבדיקה» (the owner's review booth and the laptop self-check) and `cover` «תמונת שער והדפסה». Model-free in what must be proven here; each version 1 is cut to at most three workers and one integration round. **Design and verification done the same day** (six `opus` designers, six `opus` verifiers, each verifier checked the plan against the code; plans, verifier files and the lead's `decisions.md` are under `.artifacts/wave8/`, not tracked). Decided: `booth` version 1 is a 14-card first sitting with a scrubbed report; `cover` is cut to «הפריים הכי חד» and «שמירת התמונה» (the cover sequence is not built this wave); `growing` stores NO eye marks (they travel once in the owner-only request; no new sensitive data); `audiences` is «רגועה» and «לסבא וסבתא» only (the sharing version is a later track: it could not be made fail-safe as designed); `ink` and `tripmap` share ONE new clip kind `drawn` on text tracks (no schema version bump), landed first as an inert carrier. **State at 09:50 UTC the same day:** the four pre-passes and the `drawn` carrier are merged (contracts, stubs and inert carrier only; nothing of wave 8 is offered to the owner yet); every builder of booth, cover, audiences and growing has handed back (unit tests only; no browser proof run); four integrators (`opus`) are joining them and running the proofs; the ink and trip-map pre-passes are running on the carrier. Found on the way and fixed on this branch: two tests broken by merges (`pearls.test.ts` route parameters; `instruments-wording.test.ts` after the real kit became calibrated). A builder's report of "14 flashes a second" was investigated (`opus`): the shared strobe limit does not leak (three starts a second in every case tried at seven frame rates); the number was the busiest single cell (the test video's barcode strip), while the failing area flashed 4–5 times a second because a strobe at its ceiling plus a `flash` transition (outside the light plan by ADR-0011) add up, with nothing limiting it in preview or export (the export reports, never blocks). Being built in `wt/flashq`: the report's number becomes the failing area's rate, and transitions that are themselves one general flash take a slot in the light plan. **Batch C (owner's instruction, 2026-11-26):** eight more tracks join wave 8 — `teach`, `owntemplate`, `sounddesign`, `picmodels`+`eyes`, `living`, `behind`, `grade`, `qc` — design round started (eight `opus` designers, read-only). The models these need are already in the owner's library (`da3-small/base`, `birefnet-lite`, `sam2`, `yunet`, `panns-cnn14`); none exists here, so they are proven here with labelled test adapters only and checked for real in the next wave, which the owner fixed as the laptop wave (fixes from the review booth, switching on the downloaded models, calibrating the recorded kit, speed on the RTX 3050). Also done for the owner the same day: `start-pulseframe.cmd` (update, build, start; not run on Windows by anyone) and a failed library scan said in Hebrew on the settings page (`LibraryStatus.indexBuildFailed`, unit tests only).
- **2026-11-26, later the same day (state at about 11:00 UTC).** On the owner's laptop the editor runs and speech recognition recognises Hebrew (after `PYTHON_WORKER_FLAGS`, `-I -X utf8`, for every Python worker: commit d0768a2; before it a Hebrew transcript killed the recogniser with `UnicodeEncodeError 'charmap'`). **Owner report being worked on (`opus`, worktree `wt/capfix`):** a caption look was picked but no caption shows on the picture. Read in the code by the lead, not yet reproduced: a caption track made before a transcript exists is `manual` and stays so (`CaptionControls` in `TranscriptPanel.tsx` only offers transcript mode when it creates the track), so a look styles an empty track; and the timeline lane of a transcript track with no linked track draws nothing (`Timeline.tsx` `autoCues`) although the engine captions every track then. **Merged:** the trip-map pre-pass (contracts, Natural Earth v5.1.2 coast and lake data 176,834 B, three ops, stubs; nothing offered; its three builders are running: W1 core and W2 engine `opus`, W3 editor `sonnet`); «התקנת מנוע הדיבור» (ADR pending in INTEGRATIONS; tests above; never run on Windows; the lead read the install job's process, path and clean-up code and moved its Python calls to the shared UTF-8 flags). **Machine:** about twenty agents on 4 CPUs and 15 GB had tsc and Chromium OOM-killed (load 50); every heavy command now goes through `.artifacts/heavy` (3 slots; rule added to `.artifacts/w6-rules.md` and sent to every running agent); results reported as "Killed" before that are the machine's. **Also started on the owner's word:** a design review of the whole editor at the owner's viewport (1908×876, and 125% scaling), `opus`, review only (`docs/design/DESIGN_REVIEW_2026-11-26.md` when it lands); fixes follow as work packages. **Running:** integrations of booth, cover, audiences and growing; pre-passes of ink, qc (the export-sheet loudness fix is committed there first), own template, living picture, teach once, text behind, curves and scopes, picture models and eyes, phone pickup; the proof-ports worker.
- Perform mode (ADR-0025): integrated and proven on the CPU profile (below); open: the live-path budget for 200 hits (at the edge of one frame period here), latency (not checked), the owner's calibration and review of the pad names and sounds.
- Hebrew command bar (ADR-0027): contracts and three builders merged, each reviewed and repaired — a model-free Hebrew/English grammar (36 intents, exact numbers and times: «שנייה וחצי» = 3/2 s, «דקה ועשר» = 70 s, a copied hh:mm:ss:ff lands on its own frame at 29.97 fps; ambiguous phrases ask; never guessed), the language-model path to the honest «עוזר השפה לא מחובר» state (allowlist summary, propose-only, monthly and per-request limits, key never backed up; contract-tested with a labelled test double only), and the bar inside the palette with a preview that must be seen before Enter commits it at its revision. **Integrated and proven (one run each):** `pnpm proof:command-bar` 23 pass, 5 contract-tested against a labelled MOCK adapter (credentials required), 0 fail — the owner's nine examples in the real editor in Hebrew and English, one commit each, Ctrl+Z back to the exact document hash, a stale preview asks «הפרויקט השתנה — בדקו שוב», 0 outbound connections, a planted key in none of 508 responses and no log line; editor interactions 70/70; accessibility 0 findings in both languages (and both themes on the palette); test-id inventory +24, none lost; 5067 unit tests. Faults found by the integration and fixed: the Delete key dropped selected clips off the main video track while counting them; the bar's rows were clipped by a design-system class; the assistant row was a disabled option inside the listbox; the row polled forever after an uncertain send; English server hints on a Hebrew page. Faults found in review and fixed: words like «constructor» hit Object.prototype in the word tables; a timecode copied from the editor landed on another frame at 29.97 fps; the per-request cap could be passed when the adapter's token count was not bounded by bytes; Enter did nothing on a re-planned preview after a 409.
- Commit speed (one sample each, results byte-identical — an equivalence test over 2700 fuzz steps and 230 real-store commits compares every row and event with the old path): a 299-step perform take 19.9 → 12.4 ms median (p95 34.0 → 14.1), 1000 clips 16.3 → 12.8 ms, by exact small-integer fast paths in `packages/time` (BigInt kept as the reference) and a plain-data document copy; perform's 200-hit live path now 19.2 ms worst of 10 against 33.3 ms; the keyframes proof's HTTP-level 2000-key delta is still 24.8 ms against +15 ms (recorded, not gated; the request path, not the commit, was not profiled).
- Follow-ups done (2026-11-19, verified-here): the planner names a shape hold itself (an optional, additive `why: 'shape'` on a flash_limit chip, set in `moves.ts`; the take lane words it `pv.held.shape`; `markShapeHolds` and its copy of the rule removed); the transport timecode no longer overlaps its total (the timecode never shrinks, its column is never narrower than it, the total goes under it where there is no room; the keys keep the middle column, which also centres play on a narrow stage, and a stage of 221–340 px keeps the timecode beside play): checked by a headless screenshot at 1024, 1280 and 1440 px, Hebrew and English, all ten modes (60/60, no overlap); `pnpm proof:editor-interactions` 64/64, `pnpm proof:phase3` 53/53 with the baselines unchanged (SSIM 0.993/0.994/1, none re-recorded). Still recorded: commit cost grows with the document (2000 keys 31 ms; perform's 200-hit live path at the edge of one frame period here).
- Commit cost (2026-11-19, verified-here, one sample each on this loaded 4-CPU machine): `pnpm bench:commit` times Store.commit per phase (docs/evidence/commit-bench.json, before and after). Two changes, both byte-identical to the old path: rational time computes exactly in JS numbers when values have ≤ 15 digits and products stay within MAX_SAFE_INTEGER (BigInt reference kept in `packages/time/src/exact.ts`), and applyTransaction copies with `cloneDocument` (`packages/core/src/clone.ts`, falls back to structuredClone for anything not plain data). Median commit: 1000 clips 16.3 → 12.8 ms, 2000-key replace 16.4 → 11.4 ms, the 299-step take 19.9 → 12.4 ms (p95 34.0 → 14.1); live path in Node 16.6 → 11.0 ms. `commit-equivalence.test.ts` runs the ops-fuzz corpus (30 seeds × 90 steps, takes included) and the perform fuzz through the store (12 seeds, undo/redo/replay, every table row and event) on both paths and finds them identical; the keyframe-replay golden still matches. `pnpm proof:perform` 28/28, live path slowest 19.2 ms of 33.3 (was 29.0); `pnpm proof:keyframes` 15/15, its recorded 2000-key delta 24.8 ms (was 25.3; still over +15, not gated: the HTTP/governance path and the connected editor's refetch weigh more there than the commit). Not done, on purpose: incremental validation (≤ 5 % of a commit after this; its cross-entity rules make an exact proof costly) and diffing only affectedIds (ops' affectedIds are not guaranteed complete, and the diff is the undo record). What remains per commit is mostly parse + schema check of the stored document, the SQLite write (fsync) and JSON.stringify.
- Fixed 2026-11-19: a stored media file missing on disk answers 404 `missing_media` with a hint instead of a 500 with a stack (content and render-worker routes; still and export refuse with the blocker `missing_media`; one check in `apps/server/src/mediaPresence.ts`; the editor marks such assets missing and leaves them out of the preview; `GET /projects/:id/missing-media`); the light theme passes contrast (the audit gained a light pass of 7 states; 0 findings in both themes and both languages — it found more old dark-only tokens than the two reported); perform's pad 7 note is in view at 1280 and 1440 px. Then the phase 3 regression the command bar caused, fixed: the bar's answer is the palette's first, active option, so Enter on a command's exact name ran nothing when the grammar did not know that name; an exact command name now runs its command (unless the bar has a previewed plan for it, which does the same), while a partial name stays an unknown request and runs nothing on Enter, as the plan requires (`apps/web/src/editor/palette.enter.ts`, 9 tests; the phase 3 proof now types exact names and also checks which row is active). `pnpm proof:phase3` 53/53, command bar 23 + 5 contract-tested, editor interactions 70/70, perform 28/28, accessibility 0 findings; perform at 1024 px in English fits on one line (calibrate becomes an icon in a narrow panel). Still open: at 1024 px pad 7's «הוסף לספרייה» needs scrolling; whether common partial phrases ("close empty gaps") should join the grammar is listed for the owner.
- **Full regression, 2026-11-19 (one run each, verified-here):** every proof, check and conformance run above passes on the merged tree after waves 4 and 5; ComfyUI and speech proofs not run (needs external setup). Found and fixed: the event card's actions overflowed the media drawer after the family-film button was added (lifeline-ui 26/27 → 27/27, CSS test); the input and image matrices still read notices from the card after the redesign moved them into the card's ⋯ menu (inputs 3/4 → 4/4, images 2/3 → 3/3, expected lists unchanged). `proof:creative` 41/41 on its third run alone (the strobe-hover check failed twice under load, as recorded in c78a2a3; flash safety held in every run).
- **Wave 6 (2026-11-24), known follow-ups closed, verified-here (one run each):** transcript-driven edits (delete words, filler and silence cuts, tidy-speech, scene splits, pacing) follow a clip that shows a time-preserving derived copy back to the transcribed recording and map times exactly; clips of a copy without a recorded time mapping (reversed, stretched, generated) are skipped and named, and the edit is refused (`untimed_copy`) only when nothing else plays the recording; the transcript and filler panels show the recording's transcript for a clip of a copy (`pnpm proof:phase6` 37/37, `proof:phase11` 12/12; the new panel note not yet seen in a browser). An agent can stop an export the owner's tab renders, within a grant that edits the whole project (ADR-0002 amendment; the tab stops at its next heartbeat, publishes nothing, releases its lease; `pnpm proof:browser-export` 27/27 — one sample: cancelled after 41 frames, stopped at frame 241, 4.92 s later). Toasts render inside an open modal dialog, inside its focus trap (`pnpm audit:a11y` 0 findings with the state toast-in-modal; editor interactions 73/73). Importing an archive clears every translation's reviewed mark and says so (`pnpm proof:phase12` 20/20). A picture clip's opacity now goes into a scratch buffer's alpha, as the adjustment pass's does: before, a faded picture came out on average half a level low and up to 2 off the exact mix; now every byte is within 1 of the exact mix at the nearest 8-bit alpha at all 99 opacities with the normal blend and at 14 opacities with seven blend modes (new `pnpm conformance:opacity` 3/3, real stills in Chromium; `proof:phase7` 33, parity, `proof:adjust` 11, `proof:gpu` 92, render-fuzz 2; no baseline re-recorded). Not changed: titles and time-effect taps (still `globalAlpha`), an average bias of about a quarter level from Chromium's own source-over, and five blend modes Chromium itself computes more than 1 off (hard-light, difference, exclusion, colour dodge and burn). A hand delete survives regeneration for templates, the family film and the beat montage, derived from the run's records (optional additions only, no version bump); the plan says what was left out by name, and `restoreRemoved` (REST, MCP, a «החזרת החלקים שנמחקו» button) is the explicit way back; a film whose music was deleted gets the no-music mix, with its cuts kept on the removed music's bars until the length or order changes; one deleted moment leaves out only that window when the file has another (`proof:templates` 147, `proof:family-film` 41, `proof:phase11` 12). The multicam auto-switch was already safe. Multicam live switching prefetches decoders within one memory budget (off under 2 GB reported memory, with a note): in five runs of `proof:multicam-ui` (15/15 each) all 12 cut pictures took a warm decoder over, the median cut lateness was 0.88–1.28 frames against 1.92–4.24 before, and the 2-frame p95 budget was met at load average 4–5 (1.2 and 1.68 frames) and missed at 23–26 (2.4–3.5 frames); the picture stays what the evaluator gives. Revision events carry their transaction's entity changes to the owner's session only; the editor applies them (checked by hash against the server after every event of fuzzed sequences) and no longer reads the whole project per event (`proof:phase5` 34, editor interactions 70). The 2000-key HTTP delta stays over +15 ms (bench median about 22 ms p95, the control moved as much): it is dominated by the durable SQLite commit (synchronous=FULL, about 620 KB written); lead decision: durability and exact undo are kept, the figure stays recorded, not gated. The dissolve had the same bias and is fixed the same way (each weight goes into its picture's alpha at one 8-bit level; every byte within 1 at 21 progress values including 0.5; 2 of the 348 trend-baseline hashes re-recorded — the dissolve's middle frame, checked to be the exact mix; `conformance:opacity` 4/4, `proof:trend-light` 15, `proof:trend-transitions` 15, `proof:phase7` 33). The exact fade costs about 10 ms per faded clip per 1080p frame on CPU canvases (three faded clips: 5 → 48 ms median, one sample here); lead decision, built: playback draws faded pictures (normal blend) and dissolves in a fast draft form, within 2 of 255 with zero mean (3.4–3.8 ms per faded clip at 1080p against 10–12 exact); the paused preview, stills and export are exact and identical to each other (ADR-0005 amendment of 2026-11-24; `conformance:opacity` 8/8, parity Test 1 exact 30/30, `proof:gpu` 92 — the flash guard reads the render graph, not pixels); a faded picture with a blend mode keeps the exact fill in draft too; in full quality the fill is limited to the picture's placed box (byte-identical over 54 placements). Playback composites at the proxies' scale (short side at most 540; a 1080p project plays at 960×540, was 1707×960; `pictureSize.ts`): a playing 1080p frame of three opaque video layers 20–32 → 6.2–6.5 ms, three faded 36–41 → 9.7–11.6 (`pnpm bench:playback`, three samples, load 1.7–3.1); in the editor three stacked faded 1080p clips painted 77–99 of about 144 frames before and 138–140 after; the engine is unchanged; titles are softer while playing until the pause; a 1280×720 project now plays draft from proxies. The size is the viewer's choice in Settings («התמונה בזמן ניגון»: «חלק», the default, or «חד יותר», the former rule) — a browser preference, no op; «חד יותר» on a real graphics card is unmeasured (`proof:phase3` 53, `proof` 55, `proof:perform` 28, `proof:multicam-ui` 15). Vertical proxies stay 304×540: larger ones would not make a 9:16 playing frame cheaper and would cost 2.6–3× the bytes and decode, and the change is not contained (no proxy versioning; moments and tracking read the proxy). Preview decoder seeks: the cost of a request follows from the request sequence only (a jump followed by frames is one decode that the frames continue; a jump after a jump is a lookup only); pictures unchanged (the old decoder kept as reference over seeded patterns; `conformance:seek` 7 configurations, 0 wrong frames; `conformance:start-offset` 4/4; 550 of 550 picture hashes equal per bench sample). `pnpm bench:seek` (four samples, load 5.2 → 3.6): the frame after a seek 20–24 ms → 0, seek + 5 frames 42–46 → 22–25 ms; scrubbing no slower in 3 of 4 samples with 250–256 → 143 decoded chunks; the 5 frames played right after a scrub are 3–8.5 ms slower in 3 of 4 samples (accepted).

## Dependency-aware task list (next first)

Status words used here: **blocked** (cannot be implemented or verified without something external), **implemented, not runtime-qualified**, **verified on the current profile** (this CPU-only container), **verified on the target profile** (nothing yet: no target machine exists).

Unblocked (no decision needed), roughly by value:
1. Editor: custom arrangements, vectorscope (Motion and Color workspaces exist; Color has a histogram and a waveform) (Phase 3 carry-over; target tracks, roll and slide by dragging, overwrite from the source viewer are done; markers, multi-select, marquee, J/L shuttle, the source viewer and insert at the playhead are done).
2. (done: continuous speed ramp incl. trimming, cutting and easing; pitch-keeping stretch.) Slip, reverse and transitions on a ramped clip (Phases 7–8 carry-over; constant speed, freeze frame, volume keys with smooth stretches, the draggable volume line and freehand drawing are done).
3. (done: scheduled backups with snapshot retention, verification, pruning.) Off-machine copies remain manual.
4. (done: per-format wording for manual cues and for transcript phrases.) More caption styles, fonts and effects toward the catalog targets (Canvas2D-feasible ones only).
5. (done: transient states in the accessibility audit; translated headlines for refusals.) The detail text of a refusal is English-only.
6. Inputs: real camera and phone files (HDR sources are tone-mapped to standard range at import; HDR output needs a GPU pipeline). (Bar starts are detected on regular accents and used by `fit_music` and the beat montage.)
7. (done: request budget and export quota per grant.) A `Secure` cookie option exists; a real TLS proxy deployment is untested.
8. Commit cost grows with project size (2026-11-19: ≈1.2 ms per 100 clips at 1000 clips after the time fast paths and cloneDocument; most of what remains is loading and schema-checking the stored document, the SQLite write and serialisation — see `pnpm bench:commit`).

The `pf-implementer` definition was exercised on three real tasks: each stayed inside its file scope and reported defects instead of patching shared code
(it found the undo-body 500 and the raw-JSON idempotency comparison; both fixed by the lead). `pf-scout` was exercised once, read-only, and found two citation mismatches (fixed).
No cost figures are available. No paid call or GPU wake-up has been made. One model download was made under the owner's explicit grant: 444,473,596 bytes.
